The single prospectively recorded retry completes all three original-pack native processes under the unchanged protocol and timing conditions. All actual physical ceilings, continuous thermal/CPU admission, request completion and timing eligibility checks pass. Both fourteen-GB long natural answers are exactly code-0853. Short and coding output IDs match between allocations and match the preserved earlier original run. Every earlier failed admission remains separate and contributes no timing value. No OS service or application is paused or killed. Filesystem cache remains uncontrolled, and these are directional single-process pilot observations on the owned 48-GB Mac, not another Mac simulation or statistical qualification.

The ten-GB saved-ceiling Auto recipe uses ordinary decoding and completes the existing short/context/coding workloads. It supplies the previously missing modern complete-configuration low-budget point. The fourteen-GB comparison uses streamed drafts in both arms, changing only the existing maximum prefill reservation from 1024 to 512 rows; the expert pool increases from 1635 to 1875 slots. Exact command, binary, protocol, complete plans, physical observations, all outputs and timing primitives follow.

The capped recipe misses the frozen practical advancement criterion. Short/coding generation improves by about three percent while context generation slows, and context/long-request wall time materially worsens. Retain current automatic allocation. The small long-answer generation timer is not a substitute for full-request latency. No three-pair confirmation, default change or adaptive controller is justified by this observation. The separate already-frozen native-three-bit ten-GB pilot may now use this complete eligible original baseline; it has its own stopping rule and does not inherit promotion from preparation.
